# Phase 3A Test Execution Results Register

Date: 2026-10-09
Scope: Scenario-level traceability for existing discovered tests
Execution status: Phase 3B.1A bootstrap blocker remediated and backend/API smoke revalidated on 2026-10-09; browser execution not started.

## Phase 3B.1A Bootstrap Remediation Revalidation - 2026-10-09

Bootstrap root cause: `bootstrap_demo_workspace` used `today + 15` to `today + 16` for the seeded pending leave request. On 2026-10-09 this produced 2026-10-24 to 2026-10-25, a Saturday/Sunday range under the seeded Monday-Friday shift, so legitimate validation rejected the request with zero working leave days.

Code remediation: the bootstrap command now computes the next two contiguous weekdays for the pending leave seed and preserves the zero-working-day business validation. Supplemental regression evidence is in `docs/qa/evidence/phase3b1/07-bootstrap-remediation-focused.log`.

Retest classification: all Playwright assertion failures were test defects or fixture assumptions. One product P1 defect was opened from server-log evidence: duplicate leave policy create raises backend HTTP 500/`IntegrityError` instead of a controlled validation response (`HRADM-DEF-20261009-010`). The current product also does not expose delete APIs for employee shift assignments or shift roster templates, so delete remains a documented capability gap rather than a failed test.

Recommendation: Phase 3B.3 P0 browser scope is not blocked; fix/retest `HRADM-DEF-20261009-010` in the next stabilization pass, and proceed to Payroll certification after product owner accepts the shift assignment/roster template delete limitation or schedules archive/delete behavior for a later phase.

| Runner/timebox blocker | 1 blocked batch segment | Payroll finance report test produced no terminal output during the timebox | `HRADM-DEF-20261009-015` |

Financial correctness result: core payroll calculation, input, review, output, statutory and report screens have partial fresh evidence, but Phase 3B.4 cannot certify payroll financial correctness end-to-end because the close/output/handoff lifecycle and P100 output prerequisites did not complete.

Current payroll certification status: Not certified for release. Backend/API payroll smoke remains historically green from Phase 3B.1B, but browser certification has unresolved P0/P1 blockers around deterministic lifecycle data, output/handoff evidence, and stale automation contracts.

## Phase 3B.4D Payroll Final Blocker Cleanup - 2026-10-09

Environment: isolated local QA database `backend/db.phase3b2_browser.sqlite3`, Django API `http://127.0.0.1:8011/api/v1`, local Next.js app `http://127.0.0.1:3214`, Playwright Chromium. No production data, real payment, real provider submission, or external notification was used.

Focused remediation and root-cause results:

| Blocker | Root cause | Fix / Decision | Result |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Provider retry worker | Test reused an existing delivery that had already been requeued to `submitted`; retry worker correctly skipped non-failed/non-rejected deliveries. A second issue let the local worker inherit an invalid `POSTGRES_DB` path from Playwright env. | Retry spec now creates a fresh disposable handoff/delivery, forces a retryable failed callback, schedules retry, and runs the worker against `PLAYWRIGHT_DJANGO_SQLITE_DB`. | Passed in focused rerun. |
| Finance handoff artifacts | UI readiness used only the paged artifact table. With `artifactPageSize=1`, the selected handoff falsely appeared to be missing bank advice/statutory artifacts even though DB and summary snapshot had all three. | Handoff setup now accepts selected `handoff_id`; page passes it; readiness uses selected handoff summary counts for required artifact kinds while preserving paginated artifact table. | Passed in focused rerun. |
| TDS readiness | Payroll finance handoff setup returned published payslips without render detail, so TDS readiness could not inspect tax-sheet readiness or match the disposable PDF-tax run. | Handoff setup now includes payslip render detail for `payslip_artifacts`, allowing the TDS gate to verify tax-sheet evidence. | Passed in focused rerun. |
| Artifact access/download | Test selected a non-deterministic/latest artifact. When it selected the ESS employee's own payslip, ESS download returning 200 was correct. | Artifact access test now selects a published downloadable payroll register via backend API, proving HR download access and ESS payslip-scope denial for non-payslip/register artifact IDs. | Passed in focused rerun. |
| Missing bank-account readiness | Code currently treats primary bank account as required blocker in `DEFAULT_PAYROLL_READINESS_PROFILE`; payment handoff risk supports blocking payment submission. | No behavior or assertion changed. Recommendation: calculation can proceed, approval/output may be policy-gated, bank-transfer/payment submission should remain Blocked until bank details exist. Product decision remains open for Warning vs Blocked at earlier lifecycle stages. | Open decision, not counted as false pass. |

Focused revalidation evidence:

| Batch | Evidence | Passed | Failed | Blocked/Skipped | Not Run |
| --- | --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Combined final blocker rerun | `docs/qa/evidence/phase3b4d/01-payroll-final-blocker-focused-revalidation.log` | 4 | 1 | 0 | 0 |
| Artifact access focused rerun | `docs/qa/evidence/phase3b4d/02-artifact-access-focused-rerun.log` | 1 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| Static/backend checks | `docs/qa/evidence/phase3b4d/03-static-checks.log` | 2 | 0 | 0 | 0 |

Final Phase 3B.4D functional blocker status, avoiding double-counting diagnostic reruns: 5 focused browser scenarios are Passed, 0 Failed, 0 Blocked/Skipped, 0 Not Run. Static/backend checks also passed.

The 16 missing report/UI scenarios remain count-drift records from the current executable file inventory and are not counted as executed coverage. They must be individually reconciled before final full payroll certification, but no Phase 3B.4D blocker fix silently removed or reclassified them.

Certification status after Phase 3B.4D: targeted blockers are cleaned up and focused revalidation is green. ## Phase 3B.7A Payroll Handoff Performance Remediation - 2026-10-10

Environment: isolated local QA database `backend/db.phase3b2_browser.sqlite3`, Django API `http://127.0.0.1:8011/api/v1`, local Next.js Playwright app, Chromium, single-worker execution. External payroll/provider submissions were not used.

Root cause: `/hr-admin/payroll-handoff` was requesting the finance handoff setup endpoint with full published payslip render payloads even though the handoff screen does not render payslip details. Baseline profiling showed `payslip_artifacts` contributed about 3.73MB of the 3.84MB serialized payload, 1,151 backend queries, and about 2.89s selector/build time. The performance spec reproduced the original payroll-handoff miss at 6.05s document response against the 4s budget.

Changes made:

| File | Change |
| --- | --- |
| `backend/apps/common/api_views.py` | Added opt-in `include_payslip_detail=true` for detailed payslip artifacts and `include_output_batches=false` support so browser handoff loads only finance evidence needed by that screen. Default API behavior keeps output batches for existing API callers. |
| `web/src/app/hr-admin/payroll-handoff/page.tsx` | Handoff page now opts out of unused output batches and leaves detailed payslip payloads disabled. |
| `web/src/app/hr-admin/reports/tds-efile-readiness/page.tsx` | TDS readiness explicitly opts into detailed payslip render evidence with `include_payslip_detail=true`. |
| `web/src/lib/api.ts` | Added typed query parameters for `include_payslip_detail` and `include_output_batches`. |

Evidence summary:

| Evidence | Result |
| --- | --- |
| `01-baseline-performance-budget.log`, `02-baseline-performance-samples.json` | Reproduced performance suite failure. Payroll handoff document response: 6.05s. |
| `03-api-profile-before.json` | Handoff setup payload 3,839,471 bytes; payslip artifacts 3,733,006 bytes; 1,151 queries; 2,886.86ms build time. |
| `05-api-profile-after-payslip-opt-in.json` | Default handoff setup payload 106,467 bytes; 250 queries; 127.29ms build time. |
| `13-api-profile-after-output-batches-opt-out.json` | Browser handoff request payload 68,025 bytes; 161 queries; 102.39ms build time. |
| `10-performance-after-run2-samples.json` | Full performance spec passed; payroll handoff document response 2.85s. |
| `15-performance-after-run4-samples.json` | Payroll handoff passed at 3.11s; full spec failed on `/hr-admin/payroll-providers` at 5.41s. |
| `22-performance-after-run5-final-samples.json` | Payroll handoff passed at 3.34s; full spec failed on `/mss/approvals` at 4.58s. |
| `16-functional-regression-payroll-handoff-artifacts-reports.log` | Handoff, artifact pagination/download, finance reports and statutory reports passed 11/11. |
| `17-provider-statutory-rbac-regression.log` | Provider and payroll/statutory RBAC regression passed 7/8 with 1 existing conditional skip. |
| `18-tds-readiness-regression.log` | TDS readiness regression passed 2/2 with explicit payslip-detail opt-in. |
| `19-web-tsc.log`, `20-backend-check.log` | TypeScript and Django system checks passed. |

Phase 3B.7A focused status: `HRADM-DEF-20261010-028` is resolved for the payroll handoff route. The broader `phase8d-performance-budget.spec.ts` remains intermittently red because other launch-critical routes exceeded the same 4s document-response budget in later runs; those misses are not counted as payroll handoff failures and should be tracked separately if the Phase 3C gate requires repeated full-suite performance stability.

Focused totals for Phase 3B.7A: 1 original payroll-handoff performance defect remediated; payroll-handoff route revalidated under budget in three latest post-fix sample sets after the payload optimization, with values 2.85s, 3.11s and 3.34s. Functional regressions: 20 passed, 0 failed, 1 conditional skip. Static checks: 2 passed.
